0.B.2
The Call-by-Value Calculus
dCBV

We use here an equivalent presentation of the
dCBV
embedding
(
Def.

2
) to improve readability within
the proofs:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}x^{\tt v}&\;\;\coloneqq&\oc x\\
(\lambda x.t)^{\tt v}&\;\;\coloneqq&\oc\lambda x.\oc t^{\tt v}\\
(tu)^{\tt v}&\;\;\coloneqq&\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})\,u^{\tt v})&&\text{if }{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})\\
\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t^{\tt v})\,u^{\tt v})&&\text{otherwise}\end{array}\right.\\
(t[x\backslash u])^{\tt v}&\;\;\coloneqq&t^{\tt v}[x\backslash u^{\tt v}].\end{array}

where the predicate
{\tt Bang}(t)
holds if
t={\tt L}\!\left<\oc u\right>
, and
{\tt strip}(t)\coloneqq\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}{\tt L}\!\left<u\right>&\text{if }t={\tt L}\!\left<\oc u\right>\\
t&\text{otherwise.}\end{array}\right.

In order to properly extend the
dCBV
embedding to contexts, we
introduce a refined "super-developped" embedding. We first extend the

{\tt Bang}(\cdot)
predicate and
{\tt strip}(\cdot)
function.

Definition 7

Let
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
.

The context predicate
{\tt Bang}({\tt F})
holds if

{\tt F}={\tt L}\!\left<\oc{\tt F}^{\prime}\right>
or
{\tt F}={\tt L}_{1}\!\left<{\tt L}_{2}\!\left<\oc t\right>[x\backslash{\tt F}^{\prime}]\right>
.

The context function
{\tt strip}({\tt F})
is defined as
follows:

{\tt strip}({\tt F}):=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}{\tt L}\!\left<{\tt F}^{\prime}\right>&\text{ if }{\tt F}={\tt L}\!\left<\oc{\tt F}^{\prime}\right>\\
{\tt L}_{1}\!\left<{\tt L}_{2}\!\left<t\right>[x\backslash{\tt F}^{\prime}]\right>&\text{ if }{\tt F}={\tt L}_{1}\!\left<{\tt L}_{2}\!\left<\oc t\right>[x\backslash{\tt F}^{\prime}]\right>\end{array}\right.

Let us also introduce the
{\tt func}(\cdot)
context predicate
which captures contexts with holes placed directly to the left of an
application.

Definition 8

Let
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
. The functional predicate

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
holds if
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\,t\right>
for some
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
t\in\Lambda
. Similarly for
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
,

{\tt func}({\tt F})
holds if
{\tt F}={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}\,t\right>
for some
{\tt F}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}
,
{\tt L}\in\mathbb{L}
and
t\in\Lambda_{\oc}
.

This predicates is used to detecte places where super-development must
be used. We now introduce the
dCBV
super-developped embedding for
contexts.

Definition 9

The super-developped
dCBV
context embedding

\cdot^{\tt v*}\colon\Lambda\to\Lambda_{\oc}
is
defined on contexts as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\diamond^{\tt v*}&\coloneqq&\diamond\\
(\lambda x.{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})^{\tt v*}&\coloneqq&\oc\lambda x.\oc{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\\
({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\,t)^{\tt v*}&\coloneqq&\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathsf{der}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\,u^{\tt v})&&\text{if }{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\\
\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*})\,u^{\tt v})&&\text{if }{\tt Bang}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v})\\
\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*})\,u^{\tt v})&&\text{otherwise}\end{array}\right.\\
(t\,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})^{\tt v*}&\coloneqq&\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})\,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*})&&\text{if }{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})\\
\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t^{\tt v})\,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*})&&\text{otherwise}\end{array}\right.\\
({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}[x\backslash t])^{\tt v*}&\coloneqq&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}[x\backslash t^{\tt v}]\\
(t[x\backslash{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}])^{\tt v*}&\coloneqq&t^{\tt v}[x\backslash{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}]\end{array}

For example,
(\diamond\,y\,z)^{\tt v*}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(\diamond\,\oc y))\,\oc z)
whereas

(\diamond\,y\,z)^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\,\oc y))\,\oc z)
. This
allows to work with embedding in a contextual manner.

Lemma 26

Let
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
t\in\Lambda
. Then:

({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>)^{\tt v}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\!\left<{\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})\right>&\text{ if }{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})\text{ and }{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})\\
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<t^{\tt v}\right>&\text{ otherwise}\end{array}\right.

Proof

By induction on
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

Definition 10

Let
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then
{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
is
defined as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt rmDst}(\diamond)&\coloneqq&\diamond\\
{\tt rmDst}(\lambda x.{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})&\coloneqq&\lambda x.{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})\\[8.5359pt]
{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\,t)&\coloneqq&{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})\,t\\[8.5359pt]
{\tt rmDst}(t\,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})&\coloneqq&t\,{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})\\[8.5359pt]
{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}[x\backslash t])&\coloneqq&\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\diamond&\text{ if }{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})=\diamond\\
{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})[x\backslash t]&\text{ otherwise}\end{array}\right.\\[8.5359pt]
{\tt rmDst}(t[x\backslash{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}])&\coloneqq&t[x\backslash{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})]\end{array}

Lemma 27

Let
t\in\Lambda
and
v\in\Upsilon
. Then

{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})
if and only if

{\tt Bang}((t\{x\backslash u\})^{\tt v})
.

Proof

By induction on
t\in\Lambda
:

t=y
: Then
t^{\tt v}=\oc y
thus

{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})
. We distinguish two cases:

x=y
: Then
t\{x\backslash v\}=v
thus

{\tt Bang}(t\{x\backslash v\}^{\tt v})
.

x\neq y
: Then
t\{x\backslash v\}=y
thus

(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=\oc y
and therefore

{\tt Bang}((t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
.

t=\lambda y.t^{\prime}
: Then
t^{\tt v}=\oc\lambda y.t^{\prime\tt v}
thus

{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})
. Moreover,
t\{x\backslash v\}=\lambda y.(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash v\})
thus
(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=\oc\lambda y.\oc(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}
and therefore

{\tt Bang}((t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
.

t=t_{1}t_{2}
: We distinguish two cases:

{\tt Bang}(t_{1}^{\tt v})
: Then

t^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,t_{2}^{\tt v})

thus
\neg{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})
and by
i.h.
on

t_{1}
,
{\tt Bang}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\}))
. Moreover,

t\{x\backslash v\}=(t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})
thus

t^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})

and therefore

\neg{\tt Bang}((t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
.

\neg{\tt Bang}(t_{1}^{\tt v})
: Then

t^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,t_{2}^{\tt v})

thus
\neg{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})
and by
i.h.
on

t_{1}
,
\neg{\tt Bang}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\}))
. Moreover,

t\{x\backslash v\}=(t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})
thus

t^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})

and therefore

\neg{\tt Bang}((t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
.

t=t_{1}[y\backslash t_{2}]
: Then
t^{\tt v}=t_{1}^{\tt v}[y\backslash t_{2}^{\tt v}]
. We distinguish
two cases:

{\tt Bang}(t_{1}^{\tt v})
: By
i.h.
on

t_{1}
, one has that

{\tt Bang}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
thus

{\tt Bang}((t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
.

\neg{\tt Bang}(t_{1}^{\tt v})
: By
i.h.
on

t_{1}
, one has that

\neg{\tt Bang}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
thus

\neg{\tt Bang}((t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
.

Lemma 28

Let
t,u\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
{\tt Bang}(t)
, then

{\tt strip}(t\{x\backslash u\})={\tt strip}(t)\{x\backslash u\}
.

Proof

By induction on
t\in\Lambda_{\oc}
:

t=\oc t^{\prime}
: Then
{\tt strip}(t\{x\backslash u\})={\tt strip}(\oc(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\}))=t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\}={\tt strip}(\oc t^{\prime})\{x\backslash u\}={\tt strip}(t)\{x\backslash u\}
.

t=t_{1}[y\backslash t_{2}]
: Then necessarily

{\tt Bang}(t_{1})
and by
i.h.
on
t_{1}
, one has that

{\tt strip}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})={\tt strip}(t_{1})\{x\backslash u\}
thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt strip}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt strip}((t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})[y\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}])\\
&=&{\tt strip}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})[y\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}]\\
&=&({\tt strip}(t_{1})\{x\backslash u\})[y\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}]\\
&=&({\tt strip}(t_{1})[y\backslash t_{2}])\{x\backslash u\}\\
&=&{\tt strip}(t)\{x\backslash u\}\end{array}

Otherwise: Impossible since it contradicts the hypothesis

{\tt Bang}(t)
.

Lemma 29

Let
t\in\Lambda
and
v\in\Upsilon
. Then

(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=t^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}
.

Proof

By induction on
t\in\Lambda
:

t=y
: Then
t^{\tt v}=\oc y
and we
distinguish two cases:

x=y
: Then
t\{x\backslash v\}=v
and we distinguish
two cases:

\bullet

v=z
: Then
(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=z^{\tt v}=\oc z
and

t^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}=(\oc x)\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(\oc z)\}=(\oc x)\{x\backslash z\}=\oc z
.

\bullet

v=\lambda z.t^{\prime}
: Then

(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=(\lambda z.t^{\prime})^{\tt v}=\oc\lambda z.\oc t^{\prime\tt v}
and

t^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}=(\oc x)\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(\oc\lambda z.\oc t^{\prime\tt v})\}=(\oc x)\{x\backslash\lambda z.\oc t^{\prime\tt v}\}=\oc\lambda z.\oc t^{\prime\tt v}
.

x\neq y
: Then
t\{x\backslash v\}=y
thus

(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=\oc y
and

t^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}=(\oc y)\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}=\oc y
.

t=\lambda y.t^{\prime}
: By
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, one has that

(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=t^{\prime\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}
thus

\begin{array}[]{rcl}(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}&=&\lambda y.(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}\\
&=&\oc\lambda y.\oc(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}\\
&=&\oc\lambda y.(\oc t^{\prime\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\})\\
&=&(\oc\lambda y.\oc t^{\prime\tt v})\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}\\
&=&t^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}\end{array}

t=t_{1}t_{2}
: By
i.h.
on
t_{1}
and
t_{2}
, one has
that
(t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=t_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}
and

(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=t_{2}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}
. We
distinguish two cases:

{\tt Bang}(t_{1}^{\tt v})
: Then

{\tt Bang}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
using

Lem.

27
, thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}&=&((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\}))^{\tt v}\\
&=&\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})\\
&=&\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\})(t_{2}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}))\quad\text{(\lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{lem:bang_Strip_on_Isub})}\\
&=&(\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,t_{2}^{\tt v}))\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}\\
&=&t^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}\end{array}

\neg{\tt Bang}(t_{1}^{\tt v})
: Then

\neg{\tt Bang}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})
using

Lem.

27
, thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}&=&((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\}))^{\tt v}\\
&=&\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v})\\
&=&\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\})(t_{2}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}))\\
&=&(\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,t_{2}^{\tt v}))\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}\\
&=&t^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}\end{array}

t=t_{1}[y\backslash t_{2}]
: By
i.h.
on
t_{1}
and
t_{2}
, one
has that
(t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=t_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}
and

(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}=t_{2}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}
, thus

\begin{array}[]{rcl}(t\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}&=&((t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})[y\backslash(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})])^{\tt v}\\
&=&(t_{1}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}[y\backslash(t_{2}\{x\backslash v\})^{\tt v}]\\
&=&t_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}[y\backslash t_{2}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}]\\
&=&(t_{1}^{\tt v}[y\backslash t_{2}^{\tt v}])\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}\\
&=&t^{\tt v}\{x\backslash{\tt strip}(v^{\tt v})\}\end{array}

We rephrase the
dCBV
one-step simulation lemma by specializing the
resulting
dCBV
as follows:

Lemma 30
(
dCBV
One-Step Simulation)

Let
\mathcal{R}\in\{{\tt dB},{\tt sV}\}
. Let
t,u\in\Lambda
such that
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>
.
Then:

When
\mathcal{R}={\tt dB}
:

If
{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(u^{\tt v})
:
({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{1}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{2}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{3}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>)^{\tt v}

with
{\tt F}_{1}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,

{\tt F}_{2}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
and

{\tt F}_{3}={\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})^{\tt v*}
.

Otherwise:
({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{1}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{2}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>)^{\tt v}

with
{\tt F}_{1}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
and

{\tt F}_{2}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
.

When
\mathcal{R}={\tt sV}
:

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(u^{\tt v})
:
({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>)^{\tt v}
with
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}
.

Otherwise:
({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>)^{\tt v}
with
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
.

Proof

Let
\mathcal{R}\in\{{\tt dB},{\tt sV}\}
,
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
t,u\in\Lambda
such that
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>
. We distinguish two cases:

\mathcal{R}={\tt dB}
: Then
t={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}
and
u={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>
for some
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}

and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
and thus
t^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\lambda x.\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}\right>s_{2}^{\tt v})

and
u^{\tt v}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>
.
We distinguish two cases:

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(u^{\tt v})
: Then
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,s\right>
for some
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s\in\Lambda
and thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>)^{\tt v}&=&({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<t\right>s\right>)^{\tt v}\\
&\hbox{\hskip 12.5556pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{=}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-12.5556pt\raisebox{9.16875pt}{\hbox{{\scriptsize Lem.\ref{lem: cbv embedding with contexts}}}}\hskip-12.5556pt\hskip 12.5556pt}&{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<t\right>s)^{\tt v}\right>\\
&=&{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<t^{\tt v}\right>)s^{\tt v})\right>\\
&=&{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\lambda x.\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}\right>s_{2}^{\tt v})\right>)s^{\tt v})\right>\\
&\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{1}\!\left<{\tt dB}\right>}&{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<(\oc s_{1}^{\tt v})[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>)\right>)s^{\tt v})\right>\\
&\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{2}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}&{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>\right>)s^{\tt v})\right>\\
&\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{3}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}&{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt strip}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>)\right>s^{\tt v})\right>\\
&=&{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt strip}(u^{\tt v})\right>s^{\tt v})\right>\\
&=&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\!\left<{\tt strip}(u^{\tt v})\right>\\
&\hbox{\hskip 12.5556pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{=}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-12.5556pt\raisebox{9.16875pt}{\hbox{{\scriptsize Lem.\ref{lem: cbv embedding with contexts}}}}\hskip-12.5556pt\hskip 12.5556pt}&({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>)^{\tt v}\end{array}

where:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt F}_{1}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>)s^{\tt v})\right>={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>\\
{\tt F}_{2}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})s^{\tt v})\right>={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\\
{\tt F}_{3}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond\,s^{\tt v})\right>={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<(\diamond\,s)^{\tt v*}\right>={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt rmDst}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,s)^{\tt v*}\right>={\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})^{\tt v*}\end{array}

Otherwise:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>)^{\tt v}&\hbox{\hskip 12.5556pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{=}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-12.5556pt\raisebox{9.16875pt}{\hbox{{\scriptsize Lem.\ref{lem: cbv embedding with contexts}}}}\hskip-12.5556pt\hskip 12.5556pt}&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<t^{\tt v}\right>\\
&=&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\lambda x.\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}\right>s_{2}^{\tt v})\right>\\
&\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{1}\!\left<{\tt dB}\right>}&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<(\oc s_{1}^{\tt v})[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>)\right>\\
&\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{2}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>\right>\\
&=&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<u^{\tt v}\right>\\
&\hbox{\hskip 12.5556pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{=}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-12.5556pt\raisebox{9.16875pt}{\hbox{{\scriptsize Lem.\ref{lem: cbv embedding with contexts}}}}\hskip-12.5556pt\hskip 12.5556pt}&({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>)^{\tt v}\end{array}

where
{\tt F}_{1}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>

and
{\tt F}_{2}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
.

\mathcal{R}={\tt sV}
: Then
t=s_{1}[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<v\right>]
and
u={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash v\}\right>
for
some value
v
, thus
t^{\tt v}=s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<v^{\tt v}\right>]
.
Since
v
is value then
v^{\tt v}=\oc s^{\prime}_{2}
for some

s^{\prime}_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
and by induction on
s_{1}
, one has that

u^{\tt v}=s_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}\}
. We
distinguish two cases:

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(u^{\tt v})
: Since
u^{\tt v}=s_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}\}
then, by induction on
s_{1}
,
necessarily
{\tt Bang}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
so that

{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})
. Finally, since

s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}_{2}\right>]\mapsto_{{\tt s\oc}}{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>
,
then by induction on
s_{1}
and
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, one deduces that

{\tt strip}(s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}_{2}\right>])\mapsto_{{\tt s\oc}}{\tt strip}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>)
thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt strip}(t)\right>)^{\tt v}&\hbox{\hskip 12.5556pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{=}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-12.5556pt\raisebox{9.16875pt}{\hbox{{\scriptsize Lem.\ref{lem: cbv embedding with contexts}}}}\hskip-12.5556pt\hskip 12.5556pt}&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\!\left<{\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})\right>\\
&=&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\!\left<{\tt strip}(s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}_{2}\right>])\right>\\
&\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt s\oc}\right>}&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\!\left<{\tt strip}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>)\right>\\
&=&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\!\left<{\tt strip}(u^{\tt v})\right>\\
&\hbox{\hskip 12.5556pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{=}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-12.5556pt\raisebox{9.16875pt}{\hbox{{\scriptsize Lem.\ref{lem: cbv embedding with contexts}}}}\hskip-12.5556pt\hskip 12.5556pt}&({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>)^{\tt v}\end{array}

Otherwise:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t\right>)^{\tt v}&\hbox{\hskip 12.5556pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{=}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-12.5556pt\raisebox{9.16875pt}{\hbox{{\scriptsize Lem.\ref{lem: cbv embedding with contexts}}}}\hskip-12.5556pt\hskip 12.5556pt}&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<t^{\tt v}\right>\\
&=&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}_{2}\right>]\right>\\
&\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt s\oc}\right>}&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}\{x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>\right>\\
&=&{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<u^{\tt v}\right>\\
&\hbox{\hskip 12.5556pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{=}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-12.5556pt\raisebox{9.16875pt}{\hbox{{\scriptsize Lem.\ref{lem: cbv embedding with contexts}}}}\hskip-12.5556pt\hskip 12.5556pt}&({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u\right>)^{\tt v}\end{array}

Lemma 31

Let
t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
for some

{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
,
{\tt L}\in\mathbb{L}
,
t\in\Lambda
,
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. Then there exist

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and

s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
t={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,
{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and

s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
.

Proof

We strengthen the induction hypothesis as follows:

1.

Let
t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
for some

{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
,
{\tt L}\in\mathbb{L}
,
t\in\Lambda
,
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. Then there exist

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and

s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
t={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,
{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and

s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
.

2.

Let
{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})
and

{\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})={\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
for some

{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
,
{\tt L}\in\mathbb{L}
,
t\in\Lambda
,
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. Then there exist

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and

s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
t={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,

{\tt Bang}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v})
,
{\tt F}={\tt strip}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>)
,

{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
.

1.
By induction on
t
:

t=y
: Then
t^{\tt v}=\oc y
which
contradicts the hypothesis.

t=\lambda y.t^{\prime}
: Then
t^{\tt v}=\oc\lambda x.\oc t^{\prime\tt v}
and since
t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
then
necessarily
{\tt F}=\lambda y.{\tt F}^{\prime}
for some

{\tt F}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}
such that
t^{\prime\tt v}={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
. By
i.h.
on

t^{\prime}
, there exist
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
t^{\prime}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,
{\tt F}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,
{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and

s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
. We set
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=\lambda y.{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
concluding this cases since

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\oc\lambda y.\oc{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}=\oc\lambda y.\oc{\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>={\tt F}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
.

t=t_{1}t_{2}
: Let us distinguish two cases:

{\tt Bang}(t_{1}^{\tt v})
: Then

t^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,t_{2}^{\tt v})

and
t_{1}^{\tt v}={\tt L}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}\right>
. Thus, by
induction on
t_{1}
, necessarily
t_{1}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<v\right>
for some

{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
v\in\Upsilon
. We
distinguish three cases:

\bullet

{\tt F}=\mathsf{der}(\diamond)
,
{\tt L}={{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}
,
v=\lambda x.t^{\prime}_{1}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc{t^{\prime}_{1}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{2}=t_{2}^{\tt v}
: We
set
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=\diamond
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
,
s_{1}=t^{\prime}_{1}
and
s_{2}=t_{2}
. Then
t=t_{1}t_{2}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<v\right>t_{2}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<\lambda x.t^{\prime}_{1}\right>t_{2}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
and

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>=\diamond^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>=\mathsf{der}(\diamond)={\tt F}
.

\bullet

{\tt F}=\mathsf{der}({\tt F}^{\prime}\,t_{2}^{\tt v})
for some

{\tt F}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}
such that

{\tt strip}(t_{1}^{\tt v})={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
: By

i.h.
on
t_{1}
, there exist
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,

{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
t_{1}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,

{\tt Bang}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})
,
{\tt F}^{\prime}={\tt strip}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>)
,

{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
. We
set
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,t_{2}
then
t=t_{1}t_{2}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>t_{2}=({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}t_{2})\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>\,s_{2}\right>
and

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>=({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,t_{2})^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})\,t_{2}^{\tt v})\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>)t_{2}^{\tt v})=\mathsf{der}({\tt F}^{\prime}\,t_{2}^{\tt v})={\tt F}
.

\bullet

{\tt F}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,{\tt F}^{\prime})

for some
{\tt F}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}
such that

{\tt strip}(t_{2}^{\tt v})={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>\,s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
:
By
i.h.
on
t_{2}
, there exist
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
t_{2}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,

{\tt F}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,

{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
. We
set
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=t_{1}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
so that
t=t_{1}t_{2}=t_{1}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>=(t_{1}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime})\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
and

({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>)^{\tt v}=(t_{1}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>)^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>^{\tt v})=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,{\tt F}^{\prime})={\tt F}
.

\neg{\tt Bang}(t_{1}^{\tt v})
: Thus

t^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,t_{2}^{\tt v})

and since
t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
then
either:

\bullet

{\tt F}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({\tt F}^{\prime})\,t_{2}^{\tt v})
for
some
{\tt F}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}
such that

t_{1}^{\tt v}={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
: By

i.h.
on
t_{1}
, there exist
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,

{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
t_{1}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,

{\tt F}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,

{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
. We
set
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime})\,t_{2})

concluding this cases since
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})\,t_{2}^{\tt v})=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>)\,t_{2}^{\tt v})={\tt F}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
.

\bullet

{\tt F}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,{\tt F}^{\prime})
for
some
{\tt F}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}
such that

t_{2}^{\tt v}={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
: By

i.h.
on
t_{2}
, there exist
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,

{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
t_{2}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,

{\tt F}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,

{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
. We
set
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime})

concluding this cases since
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t_{1}^{\tt v})\,{\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>)={\tt F}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
.

t=t_{1}[y\backslash t_{2}]
: Then
t^{\tt v}=t_{1}^{\tt v}[y\backslash t_{1}^{\tt v}]
and since

t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
either:

{\tt F}={\tt F}^{\prime}[y\backslash t_{2}^{\tt v}]
for some
{\tt F}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}
such that
t_{1}^{\tt v}={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
. By
i.h.
on

t_{1}
, there exist
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that

t_{1}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,

{\tt F}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,

{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
. We set

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}[y\backslash t_{2}]
concluding this cases
since
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}[y\backslash t_{2}^{\tt v}]={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>[y\backslash t_{2}^{\tt v}]={\tt F}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
.

{\tt F}=t_{1}^{\tt v}[y\backslash{\tt F}^{\prime}]
for some
{\tt F}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}
such that
t_{2}^{\tt v}={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
. By
i.h.
on

t_{2}
, there exist
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that

t_{2}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
,

{\tt F}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,

{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
. We set

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=t_{1}[y\backslash{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}]
concluding this cases
since
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}={{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}[y\backslash t_{2}^{\tt v}]={\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>[y\backslash t_{2}^{\tt v}]={\tt F}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
.

2.
Same principle as Point
1
.

Lemma 32

Let
t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash{\tt L}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}_{2}\right>]\right>
for some
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
,
{\tt L}\in\mathbb{L}
and
s^{\prime}_{1},s^{\prime}_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. Then there
exists
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,

s\in\Lambda
and
v\in\Upsilon
such that
t={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<v\right>]\right>
,
{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
\oc s^{\prime}_{2}=v^{\tt v}
and
either:

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
so that
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}
and
s^{\prime}_{1}={\tt strip}(s^{\tt v})
.

\neg{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
or

\neg{\tt Bang}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
so that
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{1}=s^{\tt v}
.

Proof

We strengthen the induction hypothesis as follows:

1.

Let
t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash{\tt L}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}_{2}\right>]\right>
for some
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
,
{\tt L}\in\mathbb{L}
and
s^{\prime}_{1},s^{\prime}_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. Then there
exists
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,

s\in\Lambda
and
v\in\Upsilon
such that
t={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<v\right>]\right>
,
{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
\oc s^{\prime}_{2}=v^{\tt v}
and
either:

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

so that

and

. From

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
. We set

. We

\cdot

\neg{\tt func}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}})
: One then

\neg{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
concluding
this case.

\neg{\tt Bang}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
:
Thus concluding this case.

.

dCBV

Lemma 33
(
dCBV
One-Step Reverse Simulation)

Let
t\in\Lambda,u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
and
\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\in\{{\tt dB},{\tt s\oc},{\tt d\oc}\}
where
u^{\prime}
is a

\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
, then

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\right>}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}u^{\prime}\quad\Rightarrow\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\exists\;u\in\Lambda,&u^{\tt v}=u^{\prime}\\
\exists\;\mathcal{R}\in\{{\tt dB},{\tt sV}\},&\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}=\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\\
\exists\;{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}},\end{array}\right\}\text{
such that }t\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}u

Moreover,
{\tt F}
is either
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}
or

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
.

Proof

Let
t\in\Lambda
such that
t^{\tt v}
is not a

\mathbb{F}
-normal form. We distinguish three cases:

t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
thus

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}\!\left<{\tt dB}\right>}{\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}]\right>\right>=:s^{\prime}
. By

Lem.

31
, one has that
t={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
for some
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda
such that
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,
{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
s^{\prime}_{1}=\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}
and

s^{\prime}_{2}=s_{2}^{\tt v}
. We set
u:={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>
so that
t\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt dB}\right>}u
. Suppose
s^{\prime}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}u^{\prime}
for some
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF

u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. We distinguish the following two cases.

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
: Then
s_{1}^{\tt v}=\oc{\tt strip}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
thus
s^{\prime}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\oc\oc{\tt strip}(s_{1}^{\tt v})[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>)\right>
.
One has that
s^{\prime}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt strip}(s_{1}^{\tt v})[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>\right>=u^{\tt v}
using

Lem.

26
thus being a

\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>
-normal form. By confluence of

\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}
(
Cor.

10
), one
finally concludes that
u^{\tt v}=u^{\prime}
.

\neg{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
or

\neg{\tt Bang}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
: Then
s^{\prime}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\oc s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>)\right>
. One has
that
s^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<s_{1}^{\tt v}[x\backslash s_{2}^{\tt v}]\right>\right>=u^{\tt v}
using

Lem.

26
thus being a

\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>
-normal form. By confluence of

\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}
(
Cor.

10
), one
finally concludes that
u^{\tt v}=u^{\prime}
.

t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash{\tt L}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}_{2}\right>]\right>
thus

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}}{\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>\right>=:s^{\prime}
. By

Lem.

32
,
t={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s[x\backslash{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<v\right>]\right>
for some
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
s\in\Lambda

and
v\in\Upsilon
with
{\tt L}={\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
\oc s^{\prime}_{2}=v^{\tt v}
and
either:

{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(s^{\tt v})
so that
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}
and
s^{\prime}_{1}={\tt strip}(s^{\tt v})
. We set
u:={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash v\}\right>\right>
so that
t\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt sV}\right>}u
. Suppose
s^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}u^{\prime}
for some
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF

u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. Since

{\tt Bang}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
then by

Lems.

27
and
26
,
one deduces that

{\tt Bang}(({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)^{\tt v})

thus
u^{\tt v}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\!\left<{\tt strip}(({\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s\{x\backslash v\}\right>)^{\tt v})\right>

using
Lem.

26
. Using

Lems.

29
and
28
,
one has that
u^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<{\tt L}\!\left<s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>\right>=u^{\prime}
thus

a
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>
-normal form. By confluence of

\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}
(

10
), one

u^{\prime}=s^{\prime}
thus concluding this case.

\neg{\tt func}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
or

\neg{\tt Bang}(s_{1}^{\tt v})
so that
{\tt F}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
and
s^{\prime}_{1}=s^{\tt v}
. We
set
u:={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash v\}\right>\right>
so that
t\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<{\tt sV}\right>}u
. Suppose
s^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}u^{\prime}
for some

\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF

u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. We distinguish two
cases:

\bullet

: Then using

, one has that

thus

, one deduces that

using

. Thus,

and therefore
u^{\tt v}=u^{\prime}
using

and

.

is a

-normal form and using

10
, one concludes that
u^{\prime}=s^{\prime}
closing this case.

t^{\tt v}={\tt F}\!\left<\mathsf{der}({\tt L}\!\left<\oc s\right>)\right>

which is impossible by since the translation produces term in

\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>
-normal form.

This detailed simulation can be used to get iterated preservations
that

Lemma 34

Let
\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\subseteq\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
\mathbb{E}\subseteq\mathbb{F}
be two families of contexts such that:

1.

Let
{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then
{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v},{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>,{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*},{\tt rmDst}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})^{\tt v*}\in\mathbb{E}
.

2.

Let
{\tt E}\in\mathbb{E}
and
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
such that either
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}={\tt E}
or
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}={\tt E}
or

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>={\tt E}
, then
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

3.

\mathbb{E}
admits a diligence process.

4.

Let
t\in\Lambda
and
u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such
that
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\prime}
where
u^{\prime}
is a

{\tt E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
, then it is also a
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
.

Then, the following properties hold:

(Normal Forms)
: Let
t\in\Lambda
, then:

t\text{ is a $\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}$-{\tt NF} }\quad\Leftrightarrow\quad t^{\tt v}\text{ is a $\mathbb{E}$-{\tt NF} }

(Stability)
: Let
t,\in\Lambda
and
u\in\Lambda_{\oc}
where
u

is a
{\tt E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
, then:

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\prime}\quad\Rightarrow\quad\exists\,u\in\Lambda,\;u^{\tt v}=u^{\prime}

(Simulation and Reverse Simulation)
: Let
t,u\in\Lambda
, then:

t\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u\quad\Leftrightarrow\quad t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}

Moreover, the number of
{\tt dB}/{\tt sV}
-steps
matches the number
{\tt dB}/{\tt s\oc}
-steps.

Proof

Let us first show that the following two properties hold:

(One-Step Simulation)
: Let
t,u\in\Lambda
, then:

t\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u\quad\Rightarrow\quad t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}

(One-Step Reverse Simulation)
: Let
t\in\Lambda
and
u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
where
u^{\prime}
is
a
{\tt E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
, then:

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}u^{\prime}\quad\Rightarrow\quad\exists\,u\in\Lambda,\;u^{\tt v}=u^{\prime}\text{ and }t\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}}u

In particular,
{\tt dB}
-steps (resp.
{\tt sV}
-steps) are
simulated by
{\tt dB}
-steps (resp.
{\tt s\oc}
-steps) with
possible administrative steps.

We distinguish the two cases:

(One-Step Simulation)
: Let
t,u\in\Lambda
such that
t\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
. Thus
t={\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>\rightarrow_{{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>
for some
{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
,
\mathcal{R}\in\{{\tt dB},{\tt sV}\}
and
t^{\prime},u^{\prime}\in\Lambda
. Since
\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\subseteq\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}

then using
dCBV
one-step simulation (
Lem.

30
), we distinguish the
following cases:

When
\mathcal{R}={\tt dB}
:

If
{\tt func}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(u^{\prime\tt v})
:

({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{1}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{2}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{3}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}
with
{\tt F}_{1}={\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
,

{\tt F}_{2}={\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
and

{\tt F}_{3}={\tt rmDst}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})^{\tt v*}
.
Since
{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then by hypothesis,

{\tt F}_{1},{\tt F}_{2},{\tt F}_{3}\in\mathbb{E}
so that
({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}
and by transitivity

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}
.

Otherwise:
({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{1}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{2}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}
with
{\tt F}_{1}={\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
and

{\tt F}_{2}={\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
. Since

{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then by hypothesis,

{\tt F}_{1},{\tt F}_{2}\in\mathbb{E}
so that

({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}
and by
transitivity
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}
.

When
\mathcal{R}={\tt sV}
:

If
{\tt func}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})
and

{\tt Bang}(u^{\prime\tt v})
:

({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}
with
{\tt F}={\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}
. Since
{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then by hypothesis,
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{E}
so that
({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}
thus
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}
.

Otherwise:
({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}
with
{\tt F}={\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
. Since
{\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then by hypothesis,
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{E}
so that
({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}\right>}({\tt E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<u^{\prime}\right>)^{\tt v}
thus
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}
.

(One-Step Reverse Simulation)
: Let
t\in\Lambda
,
u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
and
\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\in\{{\tt dB},{\tt s\oc},{\tt d\oc}\}
where
u^{\prime}
is a

{\tt E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
and
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\prime}
. By
hypothesis, one deduces that
u^{\prime}
is a
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
. By
definition,
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{{\tt E}\!\left<\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\right>}u^{\prime}
for some

{\tt E}\in\mathbb{E}
. Since
\mathbb{E}\subseteq\mathbb{F}
, then using
dCBV
one-step reverse simulation
(
Lem.

33
), there
exists
u\in\Lambda_{\oc}
,
\mathcal{R}\in\{{\tt dB},{\tt sV}\}
and
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
such that
t\rightarrow_{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}u
with
u^{\tt v}=u^{\prime}
and
\mathcal{R}^{\prime}=\mathcal{R}^{\tt v}
. Moreover,
{\tt E}
is either

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}
,
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}
or

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>
thus by
hypothesis
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and therefore
t\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
which concludes this case.

Using these, let us now prove the three expected properties:

(Normal Forms)
: Direct consequence of simulation and reverse simulation.

(Stability)
: Let
t\in\Lambda
and
u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
where
u^{\prime}
is
a
{\tt E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
and
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\prime}
. Then by
hypothesis, one has that
u^{\prime}
is a
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
and since

\mathbb{E}
admits a diligence process, we deduce that

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}{\tt ad}}u^{\prime}
. Let us proceed by
induction on the length of
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}{\tt ad}}u^{\prime}
:

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{0}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\prime}
: Then
u^{\prime}=t^{\tt v}
and taking
u=t
trivially concludes this
case.

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}s^{\prime}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}{\tt ad}}u^{\prime}
with
s^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
where

s^{\prime}
is a
{\tt E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
: Using one-step reverse simulation,
there exists
s\in\Lambda
such that
s^{\tt v}=s^{\prime}
and thus by
i.h.
on
s^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\prime}
,
one concludes that there exists
u\in\Lambda
such that

u^{\tt v}=u^{\prime}
.

(Simulation)
: Let
t,u\in\Lambda
such that
t\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
. By
induction on the length of
t\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
:

t\rightarrow^{0}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
: Then
t=u
thus

t^{\tt v}=u^{\tt v}
and by reflexivity

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}
.

t\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}s\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
: On one hand

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}s^{\tt v}
using one-step
simulation. On the other hand, by
i.h.
on
s\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}
, one has that
s^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}
. Finally, one concludes by transitivity that

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}
.

(Reverse Simulation)
: Let
t,u\in\Lambda
such that
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}}u^{\tt v}
. By contruction, one has that
u

is a
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
and by diligence
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}{\tt ad}}u^{\tt v}
. By induction on the length
of
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}{\tt ad}}u^{\tt v}
:

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{0}_{\mathbb{E}{\tt ad}}u^{\tt v}
: Then
t^{\tt v}=u^{\tt v}

thus by injectivity
t=u
and by reflexivity
t\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
.

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}s^{\prime}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}{\tt ad}}u^{\tt v}
for some
s^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
where
s^{\prime}
is a
{\tt E}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
: On one hand,
using one-step reverse simulation, there exists
s\in\Lambda
such that
t\rightarrow_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}s
with

s^{\tt v}=s^{\prime}
. On the other hand, by
i.h.
on

s^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}{\tt ad}}u^{\tt v}
, one
has that
s\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
. Finally, one concludes by
transitivity that
t\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{E}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}}u
.

Lemma 35

The following property hold:

1.

Let
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v},{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*},{\tt rmDst}({\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})^{\tt v*}\in\mathbb{F}
.

2.

Let
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
and
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
such that either
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}={\tt F}
or
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}={\tt F}
or

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>={\tt F}
, then
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

3.

Let
t\in\Lambda
and
u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such
that
t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{F}}u^{\prime}
where
u^{\prime}
is a

\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
, then it is also a
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
.

Proof

1.

By definition of
\cdot^{\tt v},\cdot^{\tt v*}
and
{\tt rmDst}(\cdot)
.

2.

By hypothesis.

3.

By hypothesis.

See
3

Proof

Using
Lem.

34
with

Lem.

35
and

Lem.

1
.
∎

Lemma 36

The following property hold:

1.

Let
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v},{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>,{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*},{\tt rmDst}({\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})^{\tt v*}\in\mathbb{S}
.

2.

Let
{\tt S}\in\mathbb{S}
and
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
such that either
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}
or
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}={\tt S}
or

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>={\tt S}
, then
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

Proof

1.

Notice that if
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
then

{\tt rmDst}({\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}})\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, and that if

{\tt S}\in\mathbb{S}
then
{\tt S}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>\in\mathbb{S}
. Thanks to these observations, it becomes
sufficient to show that if
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
, then

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\in{\tt S}
and

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}\in{\tt S}
.

By induction on
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
:

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=\diamond
: Then

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\diamond\in{\tt S}
and

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=\diamond\in{\tt S}
.

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,t
: By
i.h.
on

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
, one has that
{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v},{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*}\in\mathbb{S}
. Let us
distinguish cases:

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in{\tt L}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
: Then

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})\,t^{\tt v})\in\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(\mathbb{S})\,t^{\tt v})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
and

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=\mathsf{der}({{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*}\,t^{\tt v})\in\mathsf{der}(\mathbb{S}\,t^{\tt v})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
.

{\tt Bang}({\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v})
:
By induction on
{\tt S}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt strip}({{\tt S}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}),{\tt strip}({{\tt S}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*})\in{\tt S}
thus
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}({{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})\,t^{\tt v})\in\mathsf{der}(\mathbb{S}\,t^{\tt v})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
and

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}({{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*})\,t^{\tt v})\in\mathsf{der}(\mathbb{S}\,t^{\tt v})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
.

Otherwise: Then
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})\,t^{\tt v})\in\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(\mathbb{S})\,t^{\tt v})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
and

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}({{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*})\,t^{\tt v})\in\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(\mathbb{S})\,t^{\tt v})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
.

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=t\,{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
: By
i.h.
on

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
, one has that
{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v},{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*}\in\mathbb{S}
. Let us
distinguish cases:

{\tt Bang}(t^{\tt v})
: Then

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})\,{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})\in\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})\,\mathbb{S})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
and

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})\,{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*})\in\mathsf{der}({\tt strip}(t^{\tt v})\,\mathbb{S})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
.

Otherwise: Then
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t^{\tt v})\,{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})\in\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t^{\tt v})\,\mathbb{S})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
and

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t^{\tt v})\,{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*})\in\mathsf{der}(\mathsf{der}(t^{\tt v})\,\mathbb{S})\subseteq\mathbb{S}
.

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}[x\backslash t]
: By
i.h.
on

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
, one has that
{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v},{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*}\in\mathbb{S}
so that

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}={{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}[x\backslash t^{\tt v}]\in\mathbb{S}[x\backslash t^{\tt v}]\subseteq\mathbb{S}
and
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}={{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*}[x\backslash t^{\tt v}]\in\mathbb{S}[x\backslash t^{\tt v}]\subseteq\mathbb{S}
.

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=t[x\backslash{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}]
: By
i.h.
on

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
, one has that
{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v},{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*}\in\mathbb{S}
so that

{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=t^{\tt v}[x\backslash{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}]\in t^{\tt v}[x\backslash\mathbb{S}]\subseteq\mathbb{S}
and
{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=t^{\tt v}[x\backslash{{\tt S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v*}]\in t^{\tt v}[x\backslash\mathbb{S}]\subseteq\mathbb{S}
.

2.

Let
{\tt S}\in\mathbb{S}
and
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
such that either
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}
,
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}={\tt S}
or

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>={\tt S}
. By induction on
{\tt S}\in\mathbb{S}
:

{\tt S}=\diamond
: We distinguish three cases:

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\diamond
: Then
necessarily
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=\diamond
and thus
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=\diamond
: Same
as the previous case.

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>=\diamond
: Impossible.

{\tt S}=\lambda x.{\tt S}^{\prime}
: Impossible
by definition of
\cdot^{\tt v}
and

\cdot^{\tt v*}
.

{\tt S}={\tt S}^{\prime}\,t^{\prime}
:
Impossible by definition of
\cdot^{\tt v}
and

\cdot^{\tt v*}
.

{\tt S}=t^{\prime}\,{\tt S}^{\prime}
:
Impossible by definition of
\cdot^{\tt v}
and

\cdot^{\tt v*}
.

{\tt S}={\tt S}^{\prime}[x\backslash t^{\prime}]
: We
distinguish three cases:

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}^{\prime}[x\backslash t^{\prime}]
: Then necessarily
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}[x\backslash t]
for some
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
t\in\Lambda
such that

{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}^{\prime}
and

\oc t^{\tt v}=t^{\prime}
. By
i.h.
on
{\tt S}^{\prime}
,
one has that
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
so that

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}[x\backslash t]\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}[x\backslash t]\subseteq\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}={\tt S}^{\prime}[x\backslash t^{\prime}]
or

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>={\tt S}^{\prime}[x\backslash t^{\prime}]
: Similar to the previous
case.

{\tt S}=t^{\prime}[x\backslash{\tt S}^{\prime}]
: We
distinguish three cases:

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=t^{\prime}[x\backslash{\tt S}^{\prime}]
: Then necessarily
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=t[x\backslash{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}]
for some
t\in\Lambda

and
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
such that

t^{\tt v}=t^{\prime}
and

{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}^{\prime}
. By
i.h.
on

{\tt S}^{\prime}
, one has that
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
so that
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=t[x\backslash{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}]\in t[x\backslash\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}]\subseteq\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=t^{\prime}[x\backslash{\tt S}^{\prime}]
or

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>=t^{\prime}[x\backslash{\tt S}^{\prime}]
: Similar to the previous
case.

{\tt S}=\mathsf{der}({\tt S}^{\prime})
: We distinguish
three cases:

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}=\mathsf{der}({\tt S}^{\prime})
: Then
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
is necessarily an
application and there exists
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
t\in\Lambda
such that
either:

\bullet

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,t
:
Then necessarily
{\tt S}^{\prime}={\tt S}^{\prime}\,t^{\prime}
for some
{\tt S}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}
and
t^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such
that
t^{\tt v}=t^{\prime}
. Two cases must be
distinguished on
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
:

\cdot

{\tt Bang}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})

and
{\tt strip}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})={\tt S}^{\prime}
: (See
(*)
below).

\cdot

\neg{\tt Bang}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})

and
\mathsf{der}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})={\tt S}^{\prime}
: Thus
{\tt S}^{\prime}=\mathsf{der}({\tt S}^{\prime\prime})
for some
{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{S}
such that

{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}^{\prime\prime}
.
By
i.h.
on
{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}
, one has that

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
so that

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,t\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\,t\subseteq\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

\bullet

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=t\,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}
:
Then necessarily
{\tt S}^{\prime}=t^{\prime}\,{\tt S}^{\prime}
for some
{\tt S}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}
and
t^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such
that
{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}^{\prime}
.
By
i.h.
on
{\tt S}^{\prime}
, one has that
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
so that
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=t\,{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\in t\,\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\subseteq\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v*}=\mathsf{der}({\tt S}^{\prime})
: Same as the previous case (with
one additional trivial subsubcase)

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\tt v}\!\left<\mathsf{der}(\diamond)\right>=\mathsf{der}({\tt S}^{\prime})
: Then either
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}=\diamond

thus
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
or
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
is an
application an the case is similar to the first case.

(*)
We distinguish two cases on

{\tt Bang}({{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v})
:

{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt L}\!\left<\oc{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}\right>
for some
{\tt L}\in\mathbb{L}
and
some
{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{S}
. Let us show by
induction on
{\tt L}
that this cases is impossible:

{\tt L}=\diamond
: Then

{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}=\oc{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}
thus
necessarily
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}=\lambda x.{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}
for some

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
such that

\lambda x.\oc{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}^{\prime\prime}

which is impossible since no
{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{S}
can have a hole under a bang.

{\tt L}={\tt L}^{\prime}[x\backslash s^{\prime}]
for some

{\tt L}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{L}
and
s^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
: Then
{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}\right>[x\backslash s^{\prime}]
thus necessarily

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}[x\backslash s]
for some

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s\in\Lambda

such that
{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}\right>
which is impossible by
i.h.
.

{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt L}_{1}\!\left<{\tt L}_{2}\!\left<\oc u^{\prime}\right>[x\backslash{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}]\right>
for
some
{\tt L}_{1},{\tt L}_{2}\in\mathbb{L}
,

{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{S}
and
u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
.
By induction on
{\tt L}_{1}
:

{\tt L}_{1}=\diamond
: Then

{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt L}_{2}\!\left<\oc u^{\prime}\right>[x\backslash{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}]
thus necessarily
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}=u[x\backslash{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}]
for some
u\in\Lambda
and

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
such that
u^{\tt v}={\tt L}_{2}\!\left<\oc u^{\prime}\right>
and
{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt S}^{\prime\prime}
. By
i.h.
on
{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}
, one has that

{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}}^{\tt v}\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
so that

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,t=u[x\backslash{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}]\,t\in u[x\backslash\mathbb{S}]\,t\subseteq\mathbb{S}
.

{\tt L}_{1}={\tt L}^{\prime}_{1}[y\backslash s^{\prime}]
for
some
{\tt L}^{\prime}_{1}\in\mathbb{L}
and
s^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. Then
{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt L}^{\prime}_{1}\!\left<{\tt L}_{2}\!\left<\oc u^{\prime}\right>[x\backslash{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}]\right>[y\backslash s^{\prime}]
thus necessarily

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}[y\backslash s]
for some

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
and
s\in\Lambda

such that
{{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}}^{\tt v}={\tt L}^{\prime}_{1}\!\left<{\tt L}_{2}\!\left<\oc u^{\prime}\right>[x\backslash{\tt S}^{\prime\prime}]\right>

and
s^{\tt v}=s^{\prime}
. By
i.h.
on
{\tt L}^{\prime}_{1}
,
one has that
{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{S}
so that

{\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime}\,t={\tt F}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}^{\prime\prime}[y\backslash s]\,t\in\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}[y\backslash s]\,t\subseteq\mathbb{S}_{\scalebox{0.55}{{\tt V}}}
.
∎

Lemma 37

Let
t\in\Lambda
and
u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{S}}u^{\prime}
where
u^{\prime}
is a

\mathbb{S}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
, then it is also a
\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
.

Proof

From the observation that
t^{\tt v}
is a
\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
,
we show by induction on the length of the derivation

t^{\tt v}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathbb{S}}u^{\prime}
that
u^{\prime}
is a

\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
which concludes this case since
u^{\prime}
is a

\mathbb{F}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
if and only if it is both a
\mathbb{S}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
and

\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>\!
-
NF
.
∎

Appendix 0.C
Appendix: Proofs of
Sec.

5

0.C.1
Internal Diligence for
dBANG

Definition 11

Internal reduction

\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}
is extended to contexts
as expected:

•

either the redex occurs in a
subterm
of some internal context,

•

or the redex occurs in an internal
subcontext
, where, in
particular,
{\tt s\oc}
-redexes are only defined as

t[x\backslash(\oc u)[x_{1}\backslash u_{1}]\ldots[x_{i}\backslash{\tt F}_{i}]\ldots[x_{n}\backslash u_{n}]]
, but not as
t[x\backslash{\tt L}\!\left<\oc{\tt F}\right>]
or
{\tt F}[x\backslash{\tt L}\!\left<\oc u\right>]
.

Examples of the first case are
\diamond[x\backslash\oc((\lambda w.w)z)]\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt dB}\right>}\diamond[x\backslash\oc(w[w\backslash z])]
,
\diamond\,\oc\mathsf{der}(\oc z)\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt d\oc}\right>}\diamond\,\oc z
, and
\diamond[x\backslash\oc(z[z\backslash\oc w])]\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt s\oc}\right>}\diamond[x\backslash\oc w]

while examples of the second case are

\oc((\lambda x.x)\diamond)\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt dB}\right>}\oc(x[x\backslash\diamond])
,

\oc\mathsf{der}(\oc\diamond)\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt dB}\right>}\oc\diamond
,
and
\oc(x[x\backslash(\oc z)[z\backslash\diamond]])\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<{\tt s\oc}\right>}\oc(z[z\backslash\diamond])
but
\oc((xx)[x\backslash\oc\diamond]z)

and
\oc(\diamond[x\backslash!y])
are
\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}
-irreducible.

Lemma 39

Let
t\in\Lambda_{\oc}
.

1.

For every
{\tt I}\in\mathbb{I}
, if
{\tt I}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt F}
then
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{I}
.

2.

For every
{\tt I}\in\mathbb{I}
, if
t\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}t^{\prime}
then
{\tt I}\!\left<t\right>\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt I}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>
.

3.

For every
{\tt F}\in\mathbb{F}
, if
{\tt F}\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt F}^{\prime}
then
{\tt F}\!\left<t\right>\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt F}^{\prime}\!\left<t\right>
.

Proof

1.

By induction on the definition of
\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}
on contexts.

2.

Since
t\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}t^{\prime}
, there is a full context
{\tt F}
and
r,r^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t={\tt F}\!\left<r\right>\rightarrow_{\mathbb{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt F}\!\left<r^{\prime}\right>=t^{\prime}
with
r\mapsto_{\mathcal{R}}r^{\prime}
.
As
{\tt I}\!\left<{\tt F}\right>
is an internal context,
{\tt I}\!\left<t\right>={\tt I}\!\left<{\tt F}\!\left<r\right>\right>\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}{\tt I}\!\left<{\tt F}\!\left<r^{\prime}\right>\right>={\tt I}\!\left<t^{\prime}\right>
.

3.

By induction on the definition of
\rightarrow_{\mathbb{I}\!\left<\mathcal{R}\right>}
on contexts.
∎

Lemma 40
